Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in navier-stokes theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 669 and the invariant I(A,B) = 479 mod 4.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^479(S, F) is trivial. QED.
